%isclose.1 (Arg_0.2: c128[64,64], Arg_1.2: c128[64,64], Arg_2.2: f64[], Arg_3.2: f64[]) -> pred[64,64] {
  %Arg_0.2 = c128[64,64]{1,0} parameter(0)
  %Arg_1.2 = c128[64,64]{1,0} parameter(1)
  %eq.1 = pred[64,64]{1,0} compare(%Arg_0.2, %Arg_1.2), direction=EQ, metadata={op_name="eq"}
